SiteCMD third-party license texts

Package identities and SPDX expressions are recorded in THIRD_PARTY_DEPENDENCIES.json.
Identical upstream license files are deduplicated by SHA-256 below.
A package without an upstream license file remains identified by its declared expression.

in BoringSSL (i.e. libcrypto and libssl) when compiled, however, so
distributing code linked against BoringSSL does not trigger this license:

   boilerplate notice, with the fields enclosed by brackets "[]"

long as the Data Recipient makes available the text of this agreement

## 5. Definitions

this agreement.

5.2. "Data Provider" means any person who is the source of Data
provided under this agreement and in reliance on a Data Recipient's
agreement to its terms.

or indirectly from a Data Provider and agrees to the terms of this
agreement.

of Data, including for example machine learning models and models'

Embedded engine module licenses

Third-party licenses for local-engine.wasm

local-engine.wasm is the SiteCMD engine module built at the engine commit below
for wasm32-unknown-unknown with rustc 1.97.0 (2d8144b78 2026-07-07).
Rebuilding that commit reproduces these bytes. This file names every third-party
crate compiled into that build and carries the license texts those crates ship.
Link-time optimization drops code the exported functions never reach, so the list
can name a crate whose code the bytes no longer hold; it errs toward crediting.

engine_commit: ad25884158c13250dd1a6feaff8f0f9f6a8635b7
artifact_sha256: 9176413b6365c1cb9b31ba0b332f0b812581811cce1cd4ce01892ceb5edda405

found [here](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/b10849fbb97a2244c086991b4623ae9f32c212d0/src/strconv/extfloat.go).
That code (used if the `compact` feature is enabled) is subject to a
[3-clause BSD license](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/b10849fbb97a2244c086991b4623ae9f32c212d0/LICENSE):

copyright notice, this list of conditions and the following disclaimer
in the documentation and/or other materials provided with the
distribution.
   * Neither the name of Google Inc. nor the names of its
contributors may be used to endorse or promote products derived from
this software without specific prior written permission.

THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE COPYRIGHT HOLDERS AND CONTRIBUTORS
"AS IS" AND ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT
LIMITED TO, THE IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR
